NOTE: These are the cards for the in-game decks. The [physical decks](https://web.archive.org/web/20091009000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/arcana/359) based on these decks are inspired by them, but they are not identical.
